Let's propose final program with a hybrid:
- Fast exact evaluation and LSE/weighted gradient functions top-level.
- A "multi-start" phase: generate many candidate sequences from:
   * parent resampled to several lengths n in set.
   * parameterized smooth shapes: endpoint taper, exponentials, cosines, random Fourier with nonnegative clip.
   * low-dimensional continuous optimizers: For each shape, run quick L-BFGS on *its own length* maybe 2048/4096 to avoid high cost. Then resample best to main length.
- A "length scan": For each n in candidate_lengths, quickly optimize from parent/resample and inits using a modest LSE (low beta, few iters) and record best. Choose best n and baseline.
- A "basin hopping" phase on chosen n (or multiple lengths): maintain a population of top candidates; each iteration: pick best, random perturb (noise, Fourier perturb, mirror, split/merge, random Fourier phase), run moderate LSE (adaptive beta), update. Repeat until time.
- A "final polish" on top candidates: high-beta LSE/L-BFGS, exact active-set gradient descent, coordinate/local neighbor.

Need be efficient. Use no closures. Could store state in global lists.

But complexity of code high. Need ensure no nested functions. Can implement loops in propose_candidate, call top-level functions. def exact_pg_polish(a, max_time, step_init=1e-4, n_peaks=50):
  p=normalize(a); best=p; best_sc=score

Let's refine `optimize_sequence` to be time-aware and not use closures. It will call `run_lbfgs_lse` multiple times. Signature:
```
def optimize_sequence(a0, time_budget_s, max_beta=100000, verbose=False):
    t_end = time.monotonic()+time_budget_s
    a = normalize_seq(a0)
    best=a; best_sc=internal_score(a)
    betas=[10,50,200,1000,5000,20000,100000]
    for beta in betas:
       if time.monotonic() > t_end - 10: break
       # allocate time proportional: for beta <200 10s, else 30s capped
       alloc = 20 if n>8000 else 30? But total budget.
       a_opt = run_lbfgs_lse(best, beta, timeout_s=alloc, pen=1.0, active_topk=256, maxfun_hard=80000)
       sc=internal_score(a_opt)
       if sc<best_sc: best,a, update
    # final exact gradient if time
    if time.monotonic() < t_end-5:
       a=exact_pg_polish(best, max_time=t_end-now, max_iter=5000, top_k=100, step_init=1e-3)
    return best
```

    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    # parent
    parent=None
    try:
       p=height_sequence_1 ...
    except: pass
    # lengths
    parent_len = len(parent) if parent else 6962
    lengths = [ ... ]
    # filter near not duplicates, between 1024 and 12000
    # pre-evaluate parent at lengths
    cand_pairs=[] # (score, length, a0)
    for n in lengths:
        if parent: a0=resample(parent,n); else: a0=np.ones(n)
        a0=normalize; score=internal_score
        cand_pairs.append((score,n,a0))
    # sort by score descending? top lengths maybe lowest score
    top_lengths = [l for s,l,a in sorted(cand_pairs)[:3]]
    # Inits for each top length: parent + some other (4 each)
    pool=[] # list of arrays? We can keep best by score using `population` list of [score,a]
    for n in top_lengths:

           # quick optimize p-norm and lse low
           a1=optimize_lse_quick(a0, time_per=min(30, budget/10))
           add to population if good
    # Sort population: keep best e.g. 6
    # Deep optimize top 3 sequentially with remaining 45%
    # Basin hop with top candidates
    # Final polish
    # Return best
```

`make_inits(n, rng, parent, count=8)`:

Let's outline make_inits:
```
def make_initializers(n, rng, parent, max_count=10):
    inits=[]
    if parent is not None:
        if parent.size == n: inits.append(normalize_seq(parent))
        else: inits.append(normalize_seq(resample_seq(parent,n)))
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    # uniform
    inits.append(np.ones(n))
    # exponentials asymmetric
    for a,b in [(1,0),(0,1),(2,0),(0,2),(0.5,2),(2,0.5),(1,1),(3,1),(1,3)]:
       v = (x+1e-6)**a * (1-x+1e-6)**b
       inits.append(normalize_seq(np.maximum(v,1e-12)))
    # random Fourier with nonnegative clamp
    for _ in range(4):
         f=max(3,min(20,int(rng.integers(3,21))))
         freqs = rng.integers(1,f+1,size=f)? But independent frequencies may duplicate; okay.
         amp = rng.standard_normal(f)*(0.3/np.sqrt(f))
         phase = rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi,f)
         sig=np.ones(n)
         for k in range(f): sig += amp[k]*np.cos(2*np.pi*freqs[k]*x+phase[k])
         inits.append(normalize_seq(np.maximum(sig,1e-6)))
    # random blobs
    for _ in range(4):
         sig=np.zeros(n)
         nblob=int(rng.integers(2,6))
         for b in range(nblob):
             c=float(rng.uniform(0,1)); w=float(rng.uniform(0.02,0.3)); amp=float(rng.uniform(0.2,1.0))
             sig += amp*np.exp(-((x-c)/w)**2)
         sig += 0.05
         inits.append(normalize_seq(np.maximum(sig,1e-8)))
    # block random
    for m in [8,16,32]:
         w=rng.random(m)*1.5+0.1
         b=np.repeat(w,n//m) ... handle remainder
         inits.append(normalize_seq(np.maximum(b,1e-8)))
    # limit count
    return inits[:max_count]
```
Need no nested loops? top-level loops fine. Use `rng.integers` returns numpy int; convert. Ensure `freqs` maybe zero? start1. Good.

def exact_gradient_polish(a0, max_time_s, max_iter=20000, n_peaks=80, step_init=1e-3, ftol=1e-12):
    t_end=time.monotonic()+max_time_s
    p=normalize_seq(a0); best=p; best_sc=internal_score(p)
    step=step_init
    n=len(p)
    it=0
    while time.monotonic()<t_end and it<max_iter:
       c=fast_conv(p); S=1; cmax=c.max(); sc=2*n*cmax
       # top peaks: threshold cmax - atol? We want exact max. Use top_k; if all equal, top_k.
       # To avoid chasing only one peak if many equal, k = min(len, max(n_peaks, int(0.1*n?)))? active count maybe peaks where c >= cmax*(1-1e-6). Could be many if flat.        q=zeros; q[idx]=1/k; H=weighted_corr_active(p,q,idx) if k<=topk else fft
       grad=4*n*(H-cmax)  # sum1
       # normalized gradient? Scale maybe 4. Use step.
       for _ in range(10):
           new=p - step*grad
           new=np.maximum(new,0); Snew=new.sum(); if Snew<=0 break
           new/=Snew
           sc1=internal_score(new)
           if sc1 < best_sc - 1e-12:
               p=new; best_sc=sc1; best=p.copy(); break
           step*=0.5
       if not accept:
           step*=0.5
           if step < 1e-8: break
       it+=1
       if best_sc < 1.5? could continue.
    return best
```

def add_to_population(pop, a, max_pop):
   sc=internal_score(a)
   if np.isinf(sc): return pop
   # if len<max: append; else find worst
   if len(pop)<max_pop: pop.append([sc, a.copy()]); return pop
   worst_idx=0; worst_val=-inf
   for i,item in enumerate(pop): if item[0]>worst_val: worst=item[0]
   if sc< worst_val: pop[worst_idx]=[sc,a.copy()]

def compute_H(a, w, topk, active_factor):
    n=len(a); w_len=2*n-1
    if np.max(w) <=0: return None
    wm=float(w.max()); th=max(wm*active_factor, 1e-15)
    peaks=np.flatnonzero(w > th)
    if peaks.size <= topk and peaks.size >0:
         return weighted_corr_active(a,w,peaks)
    return weighted_corr_fft(a,w)
```

`perturb_seq`:
```
def perturb_seq(a, rng, strength=0.2):
   a=np.asarray(a,float); m=a.mean(); if m<=0: return normalize_seq(a)
   a = a + strength*m*rng.standard_normal(a.size)
   a=np.maximum(a,0); return normalize_seq(a)
```
Strength 0.2 means noise std 0.2 mean =0.2 p. Modest. For strong use 1.

`_lse_fgrad`:
```
def _lse_fgrad(x, beta, pen, topk, active_factor):
    a=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64)
    n=int(a.size)
    if n==0:
       return 1e30, np.zeros(0)
    S=float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 1e-15:
       return 1e30, np.zeros(n)
    c=fast_conv(a)
    cmax=float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
       # all zero? value penalty?
       return 1e30 + pen*(S-1)**2, np.ones(n)
    scaled=beta*(c-cmax)
    # scaled max 0; but due to floating could >0? clip?
    # np.exp(scaled) safe; if very large positive from c>cmax due to nan? no
    exps=np.exp(scaled)
    z=float(np.sum(exps))
    if z <=0 or not np.isfinite(z):
       # fallback uniform over cmax?
       q=np.ones(len(c))/len(c)
       L=cmax
    else:
       q=exps/z
       L=cmax + float(np.log(z))/beta
    H=compute_H(a,q,topk,active_factor)
    if H is None: H=np.zeros(n)
    S2=S*S; S3=S2*S
    val=2.0*n*L/S2 + pen*(S-1.0)*(S-1.0)
    grad=(4.0*n*H)/S2 - (4.0*n*L)/S3 + 2.0*pen*(S-1.0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
       return 1e30, np.zeros(n)
    return float(val), grad
```
Check if beta maybe 0? We only call beta>0. If beta small, scaled small, z~len(c), L≈mean+max? LSE with beta small not max. Good.
Penalty with S=1 no effect. If a not normalized, objective includes penalty.

But `compute_H` expects w length 2n-1. If q sum maybe not exactly1 due to normalization; fine. It computes `wm=max(w)`. If `wm` tiny? q normalized so sum1; max≥1/(2n). OK.

`_pnorm_fgrad`:
```
def _pnorm_fgrad(x,p,pen,topk,active_factor):
   ... c,cmax
   if cmax <=0 return...
   ratio=c/cmax
   # avoid negative? c nonneg
   rp = np.power(ratio, p) # p int
   pr = np.power(ratio, p-1)
   sump=float(np.sum(rp)); sumpr=float(np.sum(pr))
   if sump<=0 or sumpr<=0: ...
   L = cmax * (sump ** (1.0/p))
   q = pr / sumpr
   coef = sumpr / (sump ** (1.0 - 1.0/p))
   H=compute_H(a,q,topk,active_factor)
   S2,S3
   val=2*n*L/S2 + pen*(S-1)^2
   grad = (4*n*coef*H)/S2 - (4*n*L)/S3 + 2pen(S-1)
```
For p=2, p-1=1, `ratio**1`. For p large, `np.power` with int; for ratio 0, fine. If p is numpy int? fine. If p=1? not use.
If `coef` overflow? sumpr≤len, sump≤len, exponent≤1, coef maybe up to len? not.

But note: For p-norm, `L` is as defined (∑c^p)^(1/p), not exactly score L. For p=4 uniform n=7000, c max~1/n, c^4 sum ~D/n^4 ~1e-13, L~sqrt? L~(1e-13)^.25=1e-3.25, score value ~2n*1e-3.25 ~? Much less than exact score 2. Min optimizer will focus on different scale; still high p p=16 L close to max? For p=16, L = cmax*(sum r^16)^1/16. If r distribution with many peaks [0,1], sum~1? If many coefficients near max, sum can be large, L up to cmax*D^(1/16). D(2n)^(1/16) ~14000^0.0625=2.4. So L within factor 2.4 max. Good. p high enough.

`compute_H`:
```
def compute_H(a,w,topk,active_factor):
    n=a.size
    wm = float(np.max(w)) if w.size else 0
    if wm <=0: return None
    th = max(wm*active_factor, 1e-15)
    peaks = np.flatnonzero(w > th)
    if peaks.size ==0:
       peaks=np.array([int(np.argmax(w))])
    if peaks.size <= topk:
       return weighted_corr_active(a,w,peaks)
    return weighted_corr_fft(a,w)
```
If active_factor too high and peaks.size small but omitted mass large, H approx poor. Use active_factor=1e-4? For q normalized sum1, if threshold max*1e-4, omitted tail may have mass maybe >? If q exponential tail, okay. For pnorm high p, tail negligible. Use topk=128 and active_factor=1e-4? For flat q, peaks all >threshold; use FFT. For one active with tail mass 1e-3, omitted mass small. Good. Maybe active_factor=1e-5 to be safe; topk 256.

`weighted_corr_active` implementation:
```
def weighted_corr_active(a, w, peaks):
    n=len(a); H=np.zeros(n)
    for k in peaks:
       k=int(k); wk=float(w[k])
       if wk==0: continue
       i0 = 0 if k < n else k - n + 1
       i1 = k if k < n else n - 1
       if i1 < i0: continue
       # a indices from k-i1 to k-i0
       H[i0:i1+1] += wk * a[k-i1:k-i0+1][::-1]
    return H
```
Verify with n=4,k=1: i0=0,i1=1; slice a[1-1=0:1-0+1=2]? a[0:2] length2 reversed -> [a1,a0] at H[0:2]. For i=0 H+=a1; i=1 H+=a0 correct. k=2: i0=0,i1=2? n=4, k<n, i1=2. slice a[0:3] reversed -> [a2,a1,a0]. good. k=3<n: i1=3, slice a[0:4] reversed -> a3,a2,a1,a0. H0=a3,H1=a2 etc c3 derivative yes. k=4: k>=n => i0=k-n+1=1, i1=n-1=3; slice a[4-3=1:4-1+1=4] reversed -> a3,a2,a1 for H1-H3. c4 terms: for i=1 j=3 a1a3; i=2 j=2 a2²; i=3 j=1 a3a1. H_i = a_{4-i}: i1 a3, i2 a2, i3 a1. Correct. For k=6: i0=3,i1=3; slice a[6-3=3:6-3+1=4] [a3]. good.

def spectral_square_refine(a, iters=200, target_ratio=0.8):
   p=normalize_seq(a); best=p; best_sc=score
   n=len(p); L=2*n-1; N=1<<L.bit_length()
   for it in range(iters):
       c=fast_conv(p)
       M=c.max()
       target_M = max(0.7*M, best_sc? maybe best_M*0.99)???
       C = c - gamma*np.maximum(c-target_M,0)
       # normalize C to sum 1? If gamma too high, C sum <1; C = C + (1-sumC)*c/M? Hmm
       C = np.maximum(C,1e-18)
       C = C / C.sum()
       F=np.fft.fft(C,N)
       A=np.sqrt(F)
       # enforce Hermitian: A[0] real, A[N/2] real, for k? Principal sqrt automatically; if C real, F Hermitian, principal sqrt of Hermitian may not be Hermitian because branch cuts. Need symmetrize:
       A0=A.copy()
       if N>1:
          A0[0]=float(np.real(A0[0]))
          if N%2==0: A0[N//2]=float(np.real(A0[N//2]))
          for k in range(1,N//2+1)? vectorize:
             # We want A0[N-1-k] = conj(A0[k]) for k=1..N/2-1. Can take mean? Simpler: take real ifft of A0? Not.
          # We can compute a_complex=np.fft.ifft(A0), then take real; non-Hermitian gives complex, but if we take real, not equivalent. Could set A0 = np.fft.fft(np.fft.ifft(A0).real)? That destroys square.
```
Symmetrizing sqrt branch is tricky. For a complex Hermitian matrix F, principal sqrt is Hermitian if F positive semidefinite. But F=FFT(C) is not generally PSD (since C real nonnegative not autocorrelation, its FFT can be negative/complex with no PSD). Square root not unique and may not yield real IDFT with simple branch. Taking principal sqrt of each frequency and then symmetrize F? Not guarantee A^2=F because if set A_-k=conj(A_k), then A_-k^2=conj(A_k^2)=conj(F_k)=F_-k; if F_k originally maybe not consistent? It is because F Hermitian. A_k principal squared=F_k, A_-k=conj(A_k) squared=conj(F_k)=F_-k. So A is Hermitian. But principal sqrt for A_-k may not equal conj(A_k) due to branch cuts. We can explicitly set:
```
A = np.sqrt(F)
for k in 1..N//2-1: A[N-k] = np.conj(A[k])
```

Let's code exact polish carefully with no lambdas:
```
def exact_gradient_polish(a0, max_time_s=30.0, n_peaks=32, max_iter=2000):
    t_end = time.monotonic() + max_time_s
    p = normalize_seq(a0)
    best = p.copy()
    best_sc = internal_score(p)
    n = len(p)
    step = 1e-5
    it=0
    while it < max_iter and time.monotonic() < t_end:
        c = fast_conv(p)
        cmax = float(np.max(c))
        sc = float(2.0*n*cmax)  # sum=1 exactly after normalize? p sum1
        if sc < best_sc: best=p.copy(); best_sc=sc
        if n_peaks >= c.size:
            idx = np.arange(c.size)
        else:
            # argpartition handles k>0
            idx = np.argpartition(c, c.size - n_peaks)[-n_peaks:]
        q = np.zeros(c.size)
        q[idx] = 1.0 / float(idx.size)
        H = weighted_corr_auto(p, q, 4096, 1.0)  # use active always since q sparse
        grad = 4.0*n*(H - cmax)
        gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(grad)))
        if gmax <= 0: break
        step = 0.05 * float(np.mean(p)) / gmax
        accepted=False
        for _ in range(6):
            new = p - step*grad
            new = np.maximum(new, 0.0)
            S = float(new.sum())
            if S < 1e-12: break
            new = new / S
            c1 = fast_conv(new)
            sc1 = float(2.0*n*float(np.max(c1)))
            if sc1 + 1e-12 < best_sc:
                p = new; best=p.copy(); best_sc=sc1; accepted=True; break
            step *= 0.5
        if not accepted:
            step *= 0.25
            if step < 1e-12: break
        it += 1
        if it % 1000 == 0: print(...)
    return best
```
But after p normalize sum1; cmax; H computed with q. `weighted_corr_auto` with active_factor=1.0? In compute_H, th=max(wm*1.0,1e-15)=wm, peaks where q>wm -> only max weight(s). But q uniform over idx, all equal; th=wm => peaks all idx? Since condition > not >=, none? If all equal, w>max false, peaks empty -> fallback argmax one. Bad. For exact active, we should use `weighted_corr_active` directly with idx. So call `weighted_corr_active(p,q,idx)` because q only nonzero on idx. Good.
Line search recomputes c1 every candidate; budget small.

Could improve acceptance by using sc1 < sc not best? But tracking best okay.

Let's write `optimize_sequence` with stage plans. Need no nested functions.
```
def optimize_sequence(a0, time_budget_s, stage='deep', rng=None):
    if rng is None: rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
    t_end = time.monotonic() + float(time_budget_s)
    best = normalize_seq(a0).copy()
    best_sc = internal_score(best)
    n = len(best)
    # p plans: list of tuples (p, weight, perturb_strength)
    if stage == 'quick':
       p_plans = [(4,0.20,0.0)]
       lse_plans = [(50,0.20,0.0),(500,0.25,0.0),(2000,0.25,0.05)]
       do_exact=False
    elif stage == 'final':
       p_plans = [(32,0.10,0.0)]
       lse_plans = [(1000,0.10,0.0),(10000,0.15,0.0),(100000,0.20,0.05),(300000,0.20,0.1)]
       do_exact=True
    else:
       p_plans = [(4,0.10,0.0),(16,0.15,0.0)]
       lse_plans = [(50,0.08,0.0),(500,0.10,0.0),(5000,0.15,0.05),(50000,0.25,0.1)]
       do_exact=True
    # p norm
    for p, weight, strength in p_plans:
       now=time.monotonic()
       if now >= t_end - 8: break
       alloc = float(time_budget_s)*weight
       alloc = min(alloc, max(5.0, (t_end-now)*0.4))
       if alloc < 8: continue
       start=best.copy()
       if strength>0: start=perturb_seq(best,rng,strength)
       aopt=run_lbfgs_pnorm(start, int(p), alloc, pen=1.0, topk=200, active_factor=1e-4)
       sc=internal_score(aopt)
       if sc < best_sc: best, best_sc = aopt, sc; print...
    for beta,weight,strength in lse_plans:
       ...
    if do_exact and time.monotonic() < t_end - 10:
       max_exact = min(30.0, (t_end-now)*0.25)
       if max_exact>5:
          aopt=exact_gradient_polish(best, max_exact, n_peaks=24, max_iter=1000)
          sc=...
    return best
```
But for deep/time budget 250s, weights sum p0.25 + lse0.58 + exact. Alloc based on original budget. Good.

Manual top selection function:
```
def select_top_indices_by_score(items, k):
    # items list of [score, ...]
    used=[False]*len(items); out=[]
    for _ in range(min(k,len(items))):
       best_i=-1; best_val=float('inf')
       for i,it in enumerate(items):
          if not used[i] and it[0] < best_val:
             best_val=it[0]; best_i=i
       if best_i<0: break
       out.append(best_i); used[best_i]=True
    return out
```
No lambda. Use top-level.

Add to population:
```
def add_to_population(pop, a, max_pop=8):
    if a is None: return pop
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    if a.size==0: return pop
    sc=internal_score(a)
    if not np.isfinite(sc): return pop
    a=normalize_seq(a).copy()
    # rescore after normalize? scale invariant, but okay
    if len(pop) < max_pop:
        pop.append([sc, a]); return pop
    worst=0; wval=-float('inf')
    for i,item in enumerate(pop):
       if item[0] > wval: wval=item[0]; worst=i
    if sc < wval:
       pop[worst]=[sc,a]
    return pop
```

Basin hopping variant creation:
```
def make_variant(base, rng, alpha_range=None):
    n=len(base); r=rng
    mode=r.rand()
    if mode <0.4:
        return perturb_seq(base, r, strength=r.uniform(0.1,1.0))
    elif mode <0.7:
        # random init blend
        inits=make_initializers(n, r, None, 3)
        rand=inits[r.integers(0,len(inits))]
        alpha=r.uniform(0.5,0.9)
        return normalize_seq(alpha*base + (1-alpha)*rand)
    else:
        # mirror? reverse base with random endpoint taper? reverse score same, but with perturbation
        base2=perturb_seq(base[::-1], r, strength=r.uniform(0.1,0.5))
        return base2
```
No lambda.

def candidate_lengths(parent_len):
    base = [2048,3072,4096,5120,6144,6962,7680,8192,9216,10240]
    if parent_len is not None and parent_len >=512 and parent_len <=12288:
        base.insert(0,int(parent_len))
        # also parent//? maybe int(parent*0.85),1.15
        for f in [0.85,0.95,1.05,1.15]:
           nn=int(round(parent_len*f)); if 1024<=nn<=12288: base.append(nn)
    out=[]; seen=[]
    for n in base:
       n=int(n)
       if 768 <= n <= 12288:
          ok=True
          for s in seen:
             if abs(n-s) <= max(50, 0.03*s): ok=False; break
          if ok: out.append(n); seen.append(n)
    if not out: out=[4096]
    return out[:14]
```

def propose_candidate(seed=12345, budget_s=2400.0):
    t_start=time.monotonic()
    deadline=t_start + float(budget_s) - 90.0
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    parent=None
    try:
       p=height_sequence_1
       if p is not None:
          arr=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
          if arr.size>0 and np.isfinite(arr).all(): parent=np.clip(arr,0,1000.0)
    except Exception:
       parent=None
    parent_len = parent.size if parent is not None else None
    lengths = candidate_lengths(parent_len)
    # limit if too many by time
    if len(lengths)>10: lengths=lengths[:10]
    length_data=[]
    for n in lengths:
       if parent is not None:
          a0=resample_seq(parent,n)
       else:
          a0=np.ones(n)
       a0=normalize_seq(a0)
       sc=internal_score(a0)
       length_data.append([sc,n,a0.copy()])
    if parent is not None:
       top_idx = select_top_indices_by_score(length_data, 3)
    else:
       # prefer middle: just first 3 or fixed 4096
       top_idx = []
       for desired in [6962,4096,8192,5120]:
          for i,l in enumerate(length_data):
             if l[1]==desired and i not in top_idx:
                top_idx.append(i); break
       if len(top_idx)<3:
          top_idx.extend([i for i in range(len(length_data)) if i not in top_idx][:3-len(top_idx)])
    top_lengths=[]
    for i in top_idx:
       nn=length_data[i][1]
       if not in top_lengths: add

def run_lbfgs_common(fun, x0, args, timeout_s, maxfun_hard=120000, gtol=1e-10, pen_upper=10.0):
   if timeout_s <=5: return normalize_seq(x0)
   x0=np.clip(x0,0,pen_upper).copy(); if sum<... normalize
   t1=time.monotonic(); f,g=fun(x0,*args); per=max(1e-5,time.monotonic()-t1)
   maxfun=int(min(maxfun_hard, max(50, timeout_s*0.5/per)))
   bounds=[(0.0,pen_upper)]*len(x0)
   try:
      res=minimize(fun, x0, args=args, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':maxfun,'maxfun':maxfun,'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':gtol,'maxls':15,'maxcor':30})
      out=np.asarray(res.x,dtype=np.float64)
   except Exception:
      out=x0
   out=np.clip(out,0,1000.0); S=out.sum(); if S<1e-9: return normalize_seq(x0); out=out/S
   return out
```

This is cheap. Let's implement:
```
def make_variant(base, rng):
    r=rng.random()
    if r<0.4: return perturb_seq(base,r,rng.uniform(0.15,1.0))
    if r<0.75:
       rand=np.abs(rng.standard_normal(base.size))*0.5 + 0.2
       alpha=rng.uniform(0.55,0.9)
       return normalize_seq(alpha*base + (1-alpha)*rand)
    # random shift/reverse blend
    base2=base[::-1].copy()
    base2=perturb_seq(base2,r,rng.uniform(0.05,0.3))
    alpha=rng.uniform(0.6,0.95)
    return normalize_seq(alpha*base + (1-alpha)*base2)
```
No expensive inits. Good.

Wait c_k=S² c'k, a_i=S a'i, ∑q a'' =S H' (not S²). Let's recompute derivative of L w.r.t a: L(a) homogeneous degree 2. Euler: ∂L_i = 2L? Not; H=∑q a, degree1, ∂L=2H (degree1). If scale a by S0: L~S0², H~S0, ∂=2H~S0 correct. Our gradient formula for F=2nL/S²: ∂=2n(∂L/S² -2L/S³) =2n(2H/S² -4L/S³). We wrote 4nH/S² -4nL/S³. Good (since ∂L=2H). If a sum S, H~S, first term ~1, second ~1/scale? OK.

For p-norm derivation, L=(∑c^p)^1/p homogeneous degree2. ∂L=2(L/C_p)H_raw. H_raw=∑w a degree1. For normalized q and coefficients, ∂L=? Our formula uses H normalized degree1? H=∑q a (degree1). Coef includes L scale? Let's re-derive normalized carefully to avoid factor error.
Let c=S² c', L=S² L'. C_p=∑c^p=S^{2p} C'. w_{raw}=c^{p-1}=S^{2p-2} w'. H_raw=∑w_raw a = S^{2p-1} H_r'? H_norm=∑q a = S H' (where q sum1). Coef_K = L/C_p * ∑w_raw? In ∂L=2 (L/C_p) H_raw =2 L/C_p * ∑w_raw * H_norm. L/C_p = (S²L')/(S^{2p}C') = S^{2-2p} L'/C'. ∑w_raw=S^{2p-2}W'. Product=S^{0} (L'/C')W' constant. Good. ∂L=2 const * S H' = degree1. Our normalized formula: L = cmax * sumrp^{1/p}. But cmax=S²Mmax. sumpr, sump dimensionless. coef = sumpr * sump^{1/p -1}? Coef dimensionless. H computed with q sum1 over a (degree S). Value L in ∂L coefficient? We set grad F = 4n*coef*H/S² -4nL/S³ +... Need ∂F =2n(∂L/S² -2L/S³). ∂L = 2 * coef * H? Let's check coef should satisfy ∂L=2*coef*H, H=∑q a. From earlier ∂L=2 * [L/C_p * ∑w_raw] * H =2*coef*H, where coef = (L/C_p)*∑w_raw. We computed dimensionless coef = sumpr * sump^{1/p -1}? R_w=sumpr, R_p=sumrp. Yes coef=sumpr * sumrp^{1/p-1}. We implemented `coef = sumpr / (sumrp ** (1.0 - 1.0/p))` = sumpr * sumrp^{1/p-1}. Good. ∂L=2coefH. Then ∂F =2n(2coefH/S² -2L/S³)=4ncoefH/S² -4nL/S³. We implemented. Good.

Now LSE: L=cmax+scale/β logz. If scale=cmax, L=S² L'. ∂L=2H. ∂F formula same. Good.

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative coefficient sequence minimizing
    2*n*max(self-convolution)/(sum)^2

The implementation uses a multi-stage, multi-start framework:

1. Resample the parent sequence (when available) to several lengths and
   build diverse low-dimensional/perturbed initializations.
2. Optimize many candidates with fast FFT-based surrogates:
      - scaled LSE smoothing of the maximum convolution coefficient
      - L_p-norm surrogates
   both optimized with L-BFGS-B on the nonnegative orthant.
3. Basin-hop from the best candidates using perturb/blend variants.
4. Finish with high-resolution LSE polish and a short exact subgradient
   line-search polish.

All helper functions are top-level; no closures or lambdas are used.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def fast_conv(a):
    """Linear self-convolution using FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    need = 2 * n - 1
    m = 1 << (need - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, m)
    return np.fft.irfft(A * A, m)[:need]


def normalize_seq(a):
    """Clip to nonnegative and normalize to sum 1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    a = a / s
    return a


def internal_score(a):
    """Exact score using FFT convolution."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        return float("inf")
    c = fast_conv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * a.size * m / (s * s))


def resample_seq(seq, n):
    """Piecewise-linear resampling preserving the continuous shape."""
    seq = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if seq.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if seq.size == 1:
        return np.full(n, float(seq[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, seq.size, dtype=np.float64)
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    out = np.interp(x1, x0, seq)
    return np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)


def candidate_lengths(parent_len):
    """Choose a modest set of plausible sequence lengths."""
    base = [
        2048, 3072, 4096, 5120, 6144,
        6962, 7680, 8192, 9216, 10240
    ]
    if parent_len is not None:
        pl = int(parent_len)
        if 512 <= pl <= 10240:
            base.insert(0, pl)
        for f in (0.85, 0.92, 1.05, 1.15):
            nn = int(round(pl * f))
            if 768 <= nn <= 10240:
                base.append(nn)

    out = []
    seen = []
    for n in base:
        n = int(n)
        if n < 512 or n > 10240:
            continue
        ok = True
        for s in seen:
            if abs(n - s) <= max(40, int(0.03 * s)):
                ok = False
                break
        if ok:
            out.append(n)
            seen.append(n)
    if not out:
        out = [4096]
    return out[:12]


def make_initializers(n, rng, parent, max_count=8):
    """Return a list of normalized nonnegative initializers."""
    out = []
    if n <= 0:
        return out

    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
        if parent.size == n:
            out.append(normalize_seq(parent))
        else:
            out.append(normalize_seq(resample_seq(parent, n)))

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)

    if len(out) < max_count:
        out.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    powers = [
        (1.0, 0.0), (0.0, 1.0),
        (2.0, 0.0), (0.0, 2.0),
        (0.5, 2.0), (2.0, 0.5),
        (1.0, 1.0), (3.0, 1.0),
        (1.0, 3.0), (2.0, 0.7)
    ]
    for p, q in powers:
        if len(out) >= max_count:
            break
        v = (x + 1e-4) ** p * ((1.0 - x) + 1e-4) ** q
        v = np.maximum(v, 1e-12)
        out.append(normalize_seq(v))

    for _ in range(5):
        if len(out) >= max_count:
            break
        nf = int(rng.integers(3, 18))
        freqs = rng.integers(1, 25, size=nf)
        amps = rng.standard_normal(nf) * (0.55 / float(np.sqrt(max(1, nf))))
        phases = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi, size=nf)
        sig = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for j in range(nf):
            sig += float(amps[j]) * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(freqs[j]) * x + float(phases[j]))
        sig = np.maximum(sig, 1e-8)
        out.append(normalize_seq(sig))

    for _ in range(5):
        if len(out) >= max_count:
            break
        sig = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        nb = int(rng.integers(2, 6))
        for _b in range(nb):
            c = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0))
            w = float(rng.uniform(0.02, 0.28))
            amp = float(rng.uniform(0.25, 1.1))
            sig += amp * np.exp(-((x - c) / w) ** 2)
        sig += 0.04
        sig = np.maximum(sig, 1e-10)
        out.append(normalize_seq(sig))

    for m in (8, 16, 32):
        if len(out) >= max_count:
            break
        w = rng.random(m) * 1.4 + 0.08
        b = np.repeat(w, n // m + 1)
        b = b[:n]
        b = np.maximum(b, 1e-8)
        out.append(normalize_seq(b))

    return out[:max_count]


def weighted_corr_active(a, w, peaks):
    """
    Compute H_i = sum_k w_k a_{k-i} for sparse peaks.
    """
    n = len(a)
    H = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for k in peaks:
        k = int(k)
        wk = float(w[k])
        if wk == 0.0:
            continue
        if k < n:
            i0 = 0
            i1 = k
        else:
            i0 = k - n + 1
            i1 = n - 1
        if i1 < i0:
            continue
        H[i0:i1 + 1] += wk * a[k - i1:k - i0 + 1][::-1]
    return H


def weighted_corr_fft(a, w):
    """Compute H_i = sum_k w_k a_{k-i} by FFT for dense weights."""
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    ra = a[::-1].copy()
    conv_len = 3 * n - 2
    m = 1 << (conv_len - 1).bit_length()
    RA = np.fft.rfft(ra, m)
    W = np.fft.rfft(np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64), m)
    out = np.fft.irfft(RA * W, m)
    return out[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def compute_H(a, w, topk, active_factor):
    """Compute weighted correlation, using sparse update when possible."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0 or w.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    wm = float(np.max(w))
    if not np.isfinite(wm) or wm <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    th = wm * float(active_factor)
    if th < 1e-15:
        th = 1e-15
    peaks = np.flatnonzero(w > th)
    if peaks.size == 0:
        peaks = np.array([int(np.argmax(w))], dtype=np.int64)
    if peaks.size <= int(topk):
        return weighted_corr_active(a, w, peaks)
    return weighted_corr_fft(a, w)


def _lse_fgrad(x, beta, pen, topk, active_factor):
    """
    Gradient of scaled LSE-smoothed score with sum penalty.
    beta is interpreted relative to the current maximum coefficient,
    preventing the common entropy bias for small absolute beta.
    """
    a = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return 1e30, np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 1e-15:
        return 1e30, np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    c = fast_conv(a)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
        return 1e30 + float(pen) * (S - 1.0) ** 2, np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    scale = cmax
    beta = float(beta)
    if beta <= 0.0:
        beta = 1.0
    scaled = beta * (c - cmax) / scale
    scaled = np.minimum(scaled, 0.0)
    exps = np.exp(scaled)
    z = float(np.sum(exps))
    if not np.isfinite(z) or z <= 0.0:
        q = np.ones(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
        q /= c.size
        L = cmax
    else:
        q = exps / z
        L = cmax + scale * float(np.log(z)) / beta

    H = compute_H(a, q, topk, active_factor)
    if H is None or H.size != n:
        H = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    S2 = S * S
    S3 = S2 * S
    val = 2.0 * n * L / S2 + float(pen) * (S - 1.0) ** 2
    grad = (4.0 * n * H) / S2 - (4.0 * n * L) / S3 + 2.0 * float(pen) * (S - 1.0)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        return 1e30, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return float(val), grad


def _pnorm_fgrad(x, p, pen, topk, active_factor):
    """
    Gradient of an L_p-norm surrogate:
        F = 2n * (sum_k c_k^p)^(1/p) / (sum a)^2 + penalty(sum a - 1)^2
    """
    a = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return 1e30, np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 1e-15:
        return 1e30, np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    c = fast_conv(a)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
        return 1e30 + float(pen) * (S - 1.0) ** 2, np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    p = float(p)
    if p <= 1.0:
        p = 2.0

    ratio = c / cmax
    ratio = np.clip(ratio, 0.0, 1.0)
    rp = np.power(ratio, p)
    pr = np.power(ratio, p - 1.0)
    sump = float(np.sum(rp))
    sumpr = float(np.sum(pr))
    if not np.isfinite(sump) or not np.isfinite(sumpr) or sump <= 0.0 or sumpr <= 0.0:
        q = np.ones(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
        q /= c.size
        L = cmax
        coef = 1.0
    else:
        q = pr / sumpr
        L = cmax * float(np.power(sump, 1.0 / p))
        coef = sumpr / float(np.power(sump, 1.0 - 1.0 / p))

    H = compute_H(a, q, topk, active_factor)
    if H is None or H.size != n:
        H = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    S2 = S * S
    S3 = S2 * S
    val = 2.0 * n * L / S2 + float(pen) * (S - 1.0) ** 2
    grad = (4.0 * n * coef * H) / S2 - (4.0 * n * L) / S3 + 2.0 * float(pen) * (S - 1.0)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        return 1e30, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return float(val), grad


def run_lbfgs_common(fun, x0, args, timeout_s, maxfun_hard=80000, gtol=1e-10):
    """
    Time-capped L-BFGS-B runner. The objective must have signature
    fun(x, *args) and return (value, gradient).
    """
    x0 = np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x0.size == 0:
        return x0
    timeout_s = float(timeout_s)
    if timeout_s <= 5.0:
        return normalize_seq(x0)

    x0 = np.clip(x0, 0.0, 10.0)
    s = float(np.sum(x0))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        x0 = np.ones(x0.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(x0.size)
    else:
        x0 = x0 / s

    t1 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        f0, g0 = fun(x0, *args)
        per = max(1e-5, time.monotonic() - t1)
    except Exception:
        per = 0.01
    maxfun = int(min(maxfun_hard, max(12, (timeout_s * 0.45) / per)))

    bounds = [(0.0, 10.0)] * x0.size
    options = {
        "maxiter": maxfun,
        "maxfun": maxfun,
        "ftol": 1e-15,
        "gtol": float(gtol),
        "maxls": 15,
        "maxcor": 30
    }

    try:
        res = minimize(
            fun, x0,
            args=args,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            bounds=bounds,
            options=options
        )
        out = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        out = x0.copy()

    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(out))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return normalize_seq(x0)
    return out / s


def run_lbfgs_lse(a0, beta, timeout_s, pen=1.0, topk=180, active_factor=1e-4, gtol=1e-10):
    a0 = normalize_seq(a0)
    args = (float(beta), float(pen), int(topk), float(active_factor))
    return run_lbfgs_common(_lse_fgrad, a0, args, timeout_s, maxfun_hard=80000, gtol=gtol)


def run_lbfgs_pnorm(a0, p, timeout_s, pen=1.0, topk=180, active_factor=1e-4, gtol=1e-10):
    a0 = normalize_seq(a0)
    args = (float(p), float(pen), int(topk), float(active_factor))
    return run_lbfgs_common(_pnorm_fgrad, a0, args, timeout_s, maxfun_hard=80000, gtol=gtol)


def perturb_seq(a, rng, strength=0.2):
    """Relative Gaussian perturbation followed by projection/normalization."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    mean = float(np.mean(a))
    if not np.isfinite(mean) or mean <= 0.0:
        return normalize_seq(a)
    b = a + float(strength) * mean * rng.standard_normal(a.size)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    return normalize_seq(b)


def exact_gradient_polish(a0, max_time_s=30.0, n_peaks=32, max_iter=800):
    """
    Short project-subgradient polish using the exact maximum peak set.
    """
    t_end = time.monotonic() + float(max_time_s)
    if max_time_s <= 1.0:
        return normalize_seq(a0)

    p = normalize_seq(a0)
    best = p.copy()
    best_sc = internal_score(p)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return best
    step = 1e-6
    it = 0

    while it < max_iter and time.monotonic() < t_end:
        c = fast_conv(p)
        cmax = float(np.max(c))
        if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
            break
        sc = float(2.0 * n * cmax)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best = p.copy()

        k = min(int(n_peaks), c.size)
        if k <= 0:
            break
        if k >= c.size:
            idx = np.arange(c.size, dtype=np.int64)
        else:
            idx = np.argpartition(c, c.size - k)[-k:]
        q = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
        q[idx] = 1.0 / float(idx.size)
        H = weighted_corr_active(p, q, idx)
        grad = 4.0 * n * (H - cmax)

        gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(grad)))
        if not np.isfinite(gmax) or gmax <= 1e-30:
            break

        step = 0.06 * float(np.mean(p)) / gmax
        accepted = False
        for _ in range(6):
            newp = p - step * grad
            newp = np.maximum(newp, 0.0)
            snew = float(np.sum(newp))
            if not np.isfinite(snew) or snew <= 1e-12:
                break
            newp = newp / snew
            c1 = fast_conv(newp)
            sc1 = float(2.0 * n * float(np.max(c1)))
            if np.isfinite(sc1) and sc1 + 1e-12 < best_sc:
                p = newp
                best_sc = sc1
                best = p.copy()
                accepted = True
                break
            step *= 0.5

        if not accepted:
            step *= 0.25
            if step <= 1e-14:
                break
        it += 1
        if it % 500 == 0:
            print(f"    exact-polish it={it} score={best_sc:.8f}")

    return best


def optimize_sequence(a0, time_budget_s, stage="deep", rng=None):
    """
    Run a sequence of surrogate optimizations starting from a0.
    Only exact-score improvements are kept.
    """
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(98765)
    t_end = time.monotonic() + float(time_budget_s)
    if time_budget_s <= 1.0:
        return normalize_seq(a0)

    best = normalize_seq(a0).copy()
    best_sc = internal_score(best)
    n = best.size
    if n == 0:
        return best

    if stage == "quick":
        p_plans = [(4.0, 0.20, 0.0)]
        lse_plans = [(15.0, 0.18, 0.0), (80.0, 0.22, 0.0), (800.0, 0.24, 0.05)]
        do_exact = False
        exact_peak = 16
    elif stage == "final":
        p_plans = [(32.0, 0.07, 0.0)]
        lse_plans = [
            (15.0, 0.07, 0.0),
            (80.0, 0.08, 0.0),
            (700.0, 0.10, 0.05),
            (8000.0, 0.14, 0.10),
            (100000.0, 0.18, 0.15),
            (600000.0, 0.18, 0.20)
        ]
        do_exact = True
        exact_peak = 48
    else:
        p_plans = [(4.0, 0.08, 0.0), (16.0, 0.12, 0.0)]
        lse_plans = [
            (15.0, 0.08, 0.0),
            (80.0, 0.10, 0.0),
            (700.0, 0.14, 0.05),
            (8000.0, 0.20, 0.10),
            (100000.0, 0.24, 0.15)
        ]
        do_exact = True
        exact_peak = 32

    for p, weight, strength in p_plans:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= t_end - 8.0:
            break
        alloc = float(time_budget_s) * float(weight)
        alloc = min(alloc, max(6.0, (t_end - now) * 0.4))
        if alloc < 8.0:
            continue
        start = best.copy()
        if strength > 0.0:
            start = perturb_seq(best, rng, strength)
        aopt = run_lbfgs_pnorm(start, max(2.0, float(p)), alloc, pen=1.0, topk=200,
                               active_factor=1e-4, gtol=1e-10)
        sc = internal_score(aopt)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best = aopt.copy()
            print(f"  {stage} p={p:g}: {best_sc:.8f}")

    for beta, weight, strength in lse_plans:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= t_end - 8.0:
            break
        alloc = float(time_budget_s) * float(weight)
        alloc = min(alloc, max(6.0, (t_end - now) * 0.4))
        if alloc < 8.0:
            continue
        start = best.copy()
        if strength > 0.0:
            start = perturb_seq(best, rng, strength)
        aopt = run_lbfgs_lse(start, float(beta), alloc, pen=1.0, topk=220,
                             active_factor=1e-4, gtol=1e-10)
        sc = internal_score(aopt)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best = aopt.copy()
            print(f"  {stage} lse={beta:g}: {best_sc:.8f}")

    if do_exact and time.monotonic() < t_end - 10.0:
        now = time.monotonic()
        max_exact = min(45.0, max(5.0, (t_end - now) * 0.25))
        if max_exact > 5.0:
            aopt = exact_gradient_polish(best, max_exact, n_peaks=exact_peak, max_iter=800)
            sc = internal_score(aopt)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best = aopt.copy()
                print(f"  {stage} exact-polish: {best_sc:.8f}")

    return best


def index_of_best(pop):
    """Return index of the lowest score in population list of [score, array]."""
    if not pop:
        return -1
    bi = 0
    bv = pop[0][0]
    for i in range(1, len(pop)):
        if pop[i][0] < bv:
            bv = pop[i][0]
            bi = i
    return bi


def select_top_indices_by_score(items, k):
    """Manually select k lowest-scoring item indices without lambda."""
    out = []
    if len(items) == 0 or k <= 0:
        return out
    used = [False] * len(items)
    for _ in range(min(k, len(items))):
        bi = -1
        bv = float("inf")
        for i in range(len(items)):
            if not used[i] and items[i][0] < bv:
                bv = items[i][0]
                bi = i
        if bi < 0:
            break
        out.append(bi)
        used[bi] = True
    return out


def add_to_population(pop, a, max_pop=8):
    """Insert/replace to keep the best candidates."""
    if a is None:
        return pop
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return pop
    sc = internal_score(a)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return pop
    a = normalize_seq(a).copy()
    if len(pop) < max_pop:
        pop.append([sc, a])
        return pop
    worst = 0
    wv = -float("inf")
    for i in range(len(pop)):
        if pop[i][0] > wv:
            wv = pop[i][0]
            worst = i
    if sc < wv:
        pop[worst] = [sc, a]
    return pop


def make_variant(base, rng):
    """Create a basin-hopping variant of base."""
    base = np.asarray(base, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    r = float(rng.random())
    if r < 0.35:
        return perturb_seq(base, rng, float(rng.uniform(0.15, 1.0)))
    if r < 0.70:
        rand = np.abs(rng.standard_normal(base.size)) * 0.6 + 0.15
        alpha = float(rng.uniform(0.55, 0.90))
        out = alpha * base + (1.0 - alpha) * rand
        return normalize_seq(out)
    base2 = base[::-1].copy()
    base2 = perturb_seq(base2, rng, float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.35)))
    alpha = float(rng.uniform(0.60, 0.95))
    out = alpha * base + (1.0 - alpha) * base2
    return normalize_seq(out)


def emergency_sequence(n=6962):
    return [1.0 / float(n) for _ in range(n)]


def propose_candidate(seed=20250610, budget_s=2400.0):
    """
    Main search routine. Returns a list of nonnegative floats.
    """
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = float(budget_s)
    deadline = t_start + total_budget - 90.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    parent = None
    try:
        p = height_sequence_1
        if p is not None:
            arr = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if arr.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
                parent = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    parent_len = int(parent.size) if parent is not None else None
    lengths = candidate_lengths(parent_len)

    length_data = []
    for n in lengths:
        if parent is not None:
            a0 = resample_seq(parent, n)
        else:
            a0 = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        a0 = normalize_seq(a0)
        sc = internal_score(a0)
        length_data.append([sc, int(n), a0])

    if parent is not None:
        top_len_idx = select_top_indices_by_score(length_data, 3)
    else:
        desired = [6962, 5120, 4096, 8192, 6144]
        top_len_idx = []
        for d in desired:
            if len(top_len_idx) >= 3:
                break
            for i in range(len(length_data)):
                if length_data[i][1] == d and i not in top_len_idx:
                    top_len_idx.append(i)
                    break
        if len(top_len_idx) < 3:
            for i in range(len(length_data)):
                if i not in top_len_idx:
                    top_len_idx.append(i)
                if len(top_len_idx) >= 3:
                    break
        if not top_len_idx:
            top_len_idx = [0]

    top_lengths = []
    for i in top_len_idx:
        nn = length_data[i][1]
        if nn not in top_lengths:
            top_lengths.append(nn)

    print(f"Starting search. lengths={lengths}, top={top_lengths}")

    pop = []
    max_pop = 8
    for i in top_len_idx:
        add_to_population(pop, length_data[i][2], max_pop)

    quick_list = []
    for L in top_lengths:
        inits = make_initializers(L, rng, parent, max_count=4)
        for a0 in inits[:3]:
            quick_list.append((L, a0))
    quick_total = min(420.0, 0.18 * (total_budget - 90.0))
    quick_per = max(8.0, min(45.0, quick_total / max(1, len(quick_list))))

    print(f"\n=== Quick multi-start ({len(quick_list)} candidates, ~{quick_per:.0f}s each) ===")
    for L, a0 in quick_list:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now + quick_per > deadline - 450.0:
            break
        aopt = optimize_sequence(a0, quick_per, stage="quick", rng=rng)
        add_to_population(pop, aopt, max_pop)

    if not pop and parent is not None:
        add_to_population(pop, normalize_seq(parent), max_pop)
    elif not pop:
        add_to_population(pop, np.ones(4096, dtype=np.float64), max_pop)

    top_idx = select_top_indices_by_score(pop, 3)
    deep_total = min(900.0, 0.32 * (total_budget - 90.0))
    deep_per = max(70.0, min(350.0, deep_total / max(1, len(top_idx))))

    print(f"\n=== Deep optimization of top {len(top_idx)} candidates ===")
    for i in top_idx:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now + deep_per > deadline - 420.0:
            break
        aopt = optimize_sequence(pop[i][1], deep_per, stage="deep", rng=rng)
        add_to_population(pop, aopt, max_pop)
        if pop:
            print(f"  deep candidate pool-best={index_of_best(pop).__class__.__name__}: score={pop[index_of_best(pop)][0]:.8f}")

    best_now_idx = index_of_best(pop)
    if best_now_idx >= 0:
        print(f"  After deep: best={pop[best_now_idx][0]:.8f}")

    remaining = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    final_reserve = max(180.0, 0.15 * remaining)
    basin_end = deadline - final_reserve
    max_variants = int(min(12, max(3, max(0.0, (basin_end - time.monotonic())) / 45.0))))

    print(f"\n=== Basin hopping ({max_variants} planned variants) ===")
    variants = 0
    while variants < max_variants and time.monotonic() < basin_end:
        if not pop:
            break
        if float(rng.random()) < 0.55:
            base = pop[index_of_best(pop)][1].copy()
        else:
            base = pop[int(rng.integers(0, len(pop)))][1].copy()
        v = make_variant(base, rng)
        alloc = max(15.0, min(95.0, (basin_end - time.monotonic()) * 0.18))
        if time.monotonic() + alloc > basin_end:
            break
        v = optimize_sequence(v, alloc, stage="quick", rng=rng)
        add_to_population(pop, v, max_pop)
        variants += 1

    if pop:
        best = pop[index_of_best(pop)][1].copy()
    elif parent is not None:
        best = normalize_seq(parent).copy()
    else:
        best = normalize_seq(np.ones(4096, dtype=np.float64)).copy()

    final_budget = max(30.0, min(max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.92, 1100.0))
    print(f"\n=== Final polish: budget={final_budget:.0f}s, current best={internal_score(best):.8f} ===")
    best = optimize_sequence(best, final_budget, stage="final", rng=rng)

    # One last very targeted exact polish if time remains.
    if deadline - time.monotonic() > 20.0:
        aopt = exact_gradient_polish(
            best,
            max_time_s=min(45.0, max(5.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 5.0)),
            n_peaks=64,
            max_iter=900
        )
        sc1 = internal_score(aopt)
        sc0 = internal_score(best)
        if np.isfinite(sc1) and sc1 < sc0:
            best = aopt
            print(f"  final exact-polish improved to {sc1:.8f}")

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(best)) or float(np.sum(np.clip(best, 0, 1000))) < 0.01:
        best = np.asarray(emergency_sequence(6962), dtype=np.float64)

    out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in best]
    if len(out) == 0 or sum(out) < 0.01:
        out = emergency_sequence(6962)

    verified = float("inf")
    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    except Exception:
        verified = float("inf")

    if not np.isfinite(verified) and parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
        out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in parent]
        try:
            verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
        except Exception:
            verified = float("inf")

    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
    print(f"\nFINAL: internal={internal_score(np.asarray(out, dtype=np.float64)):.8f}, verified={verified:.8f}")
    print(f"Length={len(out)}, elapsed={elapsed:.1f}s / {total_budget:.1f}s")
    return out
```
